## Trace the renewable-ingredient save-load regression (2026-10-13)
- The user clarified that an older working version mixed renewable ground pickups with World Loot, so a plant could yield an upgrade material. Correct the previous progress note: that confirmed historical behavior is distinct from the later Materials diagnostic that changed a map actor class without changing its template/reward.
- The catalog supports the user's explanation: all 2,253 audited renewable pickup rows point to rewards stored as `BLUEPRINT_CDO_ARTICLE_BULK`; the shared `Default__` CDO owns the `ArticleBulkInfo` reward for every physical instance of that class. The existing CDO audit includes Common Stone with 77 physical instances. This is the class/schema-preserving route that can produce the requested global type mapping.
- The current `material_generation.py` path instead patches each placed map actor's `ClassIndex` and `TemplateIndex`. Save-load `Bad export index` errors began with these generated class/template swaps; package reopen validation only proved that UAssetAPI could reopen the package, not that the game could deserialize the actor instances.
- A one-package test on a staged copy confirmed the current generic World Loot serializer does not yet handle this Blueprint CDO directly: it reopens export 2 as `RawExport` because the helper has not registered the Blueprint's own generated-class schema. So restoring the old behavior requires a CDO-aware serializer path, not just routing the current locations into `WorldLootSerializer` unchanged.
- Updated the design notes to record the confirmed historical mechanism and prohibit placed-actor class swaps for Ingredients. No application code, generated mod, game installation archive, or `~mods` file was changed. No test suite was run; the temporary proof-of-concept package was isolated and automatically removed.

- Confirmed the PE manifest requests `asInvoker`. A has no version strings; B reports product/file version `0.9.1.0`. Neither build is signed. This environment has no UPX, signtool, or code-signing certificate with private key and code-signing EKU.

Only the app executable and project-owned helper are signed; third-party binaries are excluded. Marked C `NOT BUILT — signing certificate unavailable`; no unsigned payload is labeled signed.
- Created local A/B ZIPs and SHA-256 manifest under `release_compare/`, plus the Markdown and JSON comparison reports in `workspace/`. No VirusTotal upload was made. A/B generation-output equivalence was not run because the packaged app has no non-install CLI path and a real run could write into the user's environment; the report records this limit instead of inferring equivalence from startup checks.
- Validation: PowerShell parser accepted the comparison build script; both packaged smoke tests passed; B's packaged data tree (48 files), icons, map, Retoc, and Oodle match their project inputs; ZIP contents match the extracted B payload and hashes. No randomizer logic or game installation files were changed.
- Release selection: promoted the tested Current/Build A payload and ZIP to `release/0.9.1` with the canonical `CodeVein2Randomizer-v0.9.1-win-x64.zip` filename and refreshed its SHA-256 manifest. The promoted release passed the packaged startup/resource smoke test. Build B and the C status note remain outside the official release folder as comparison artifacts only.

## Replace unsafe ingredient class swaps with shared-CDO reward edits (2026-10-13)
- Root cause of the repeated save-load `Bad export index`: the previous Ingredients generator rewrote `ClassIndex` and `TemplateIndex` for 2,253 placed World Partition pickup exports. Unreal can reopen those generated packages, but the game's serialized actor instances reject the incompatible export references when loading a save.
- Reworked Ingredients planning to permute reward Articles globally by original Article identity. Multiple pickup Blueprints that share one Article now receive one consistent replacement; canonical and mirror rows keep the same assignment. Blueprint parent families no longer constrain the reward shuffle because no placed actor class is exchanged.
- Generate now stages and validates the reward-owning Blueprint classes, registers each package's generated-class schema, patches only `ArticleBulkInfo.Articles[0].ArticleDataPath` on the shared CDO, and verifies the expected Article/count/notification and unrelated package data. It does not send material maps to the class/template patch path or emit changed `.umap` packages for Ingredients. Conflicting Article ownership or incomplete source mappings fail closed.
- Removed the obsolete material map class/template patch helper route, updated the UI/design/rules descriptions, and added regression coverage for cross-Blueprint shared Article consistency.
- Validation: focused material planner/generator/serializer tests passed (`24 passed`); the full Python suite passed before the final additional regression (`568 passed, 9 skipped, 25 subtests passed`); and the UAssetAPI helper built with zero warnings/errors. A real cached pickup Blueprint CDO was edited and reopened successfully on a temporary copy, with its assigned Article recognized and unrelated exports preserved. Built and smoke-tested `release/0.5.1-ingredients-cdo-fix/CodeVein2Randomizer-v0.5.1-win-x64.zip` (SHA-256 `8d3c13bf0e6f89d698080df0c44ac32126002a3a9f9286020b77e816e5e81755`). No cache, game installation, save, or `~mods` file was modified. The installer will replace the app-owned package on the same game installation after Generate & Install; a package from the unsafe build remains active until that replacement completes. Controlled in-game save-load testing remains pending.
